# I've Seen That Face Before

Journal: `ep1/quests/main_quest/q304_netrunners` · `MainQuest` · hash `1960400309`.

Journal premise: So, plan is to dupe an ex-military, international arms mogul in his own house. First, just gotta abduct and impersonate a pair of pro criminal 'runners specializin' in the long-lost secrets of cyberspace... Sound too easy? Good, 'cause they're makin' ya do it from inside a locked trunk. Nova, right?

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 89 exact journal-linked nodes in 10 resources; 15 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 13 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1286 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 170 distinct direct resource identities; 25 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
